Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the general_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 692 and the convergence rate is O(236/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the general_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^692 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 236-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the general_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 260 and the convergence rate is O(467/n^1).

For the inductive step, we decompose the general_chemistry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 467. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on general_chemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 579. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 741 with relations in degree 3.
